primitives.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults\n+\n+\n+def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Remove an issue ID from queue_order and skipped.\"\"\"\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    if issue_id in order:\n+        order.remove(issue_id)\n+    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.\"\"\"\n+    moving = set(issue_ids or [])\n+\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        return 0\n+    if position == \"bottom\":\n+        return len(order)\n+\n+    if position in {\"before\", \"after\"} and target:\n+        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)\n+\n+    if position in {\"up\", \"down\"} and offset is not None:\n+        return _resolve_offset_position(\n+            order,\n+            moving,\n+            position=position,\n+            offset=offset,\n+            issue_ids=issue_ids,\n+        )\n+\n+    return len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_relative_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str,\n+) -> int:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):\n+        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:\n+            return i if position == \"before\" else i + 1\n+    return 0 if position == \"before\" else len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):\n+        if item_id == target:\n+            return i\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_offset_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    offset: int,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None,\n+) -> int:\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0 if position == \"up\" else len(order)\n+    first_id = issue_ids[0]\n+    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]\n+    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)\n+    if current_idx is None:\n+        if position == \"up\":\n+            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)\n+        return len(clean_order)\n+    if position == \"up\":\n+        return max(0, current_idx - offset)\n+    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)\n+\n+\n+def move_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    issue_ids: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+\n+    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered.\n+    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS\n+\n+    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+\n+    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)\n+\n+    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):\n+        order.insert(idx + i, fid)\n+\n+    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+        existing_promoted = set(promoted)\n+        for fid in issue_ids:\n+            if fid not in existing_promoted:\n+                promoted.append(fid)\n+                existing_promoted.add(fid)\n+        plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = promoted\n+\n+    return len(issue_ids)\n+\n*** End Patch\n"

Returns count skipped.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    count = 0\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    skip_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)\n+        skipped[fid] = {\n+            \"issue_id\": fid,\n+            \"kind\": kind,\n+            \"reason\": reason,\n+            \"note\": note,\n+            \"attestation\": attestation,\n+            \"created_at\": now,\n+            \"review_after\": review_after,\n+            \"skipped_at_scan\": scan_count,\n+        }\n+        count += 1\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def unskip_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    count = 0\n+    need_reopen: list[str] = []\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+        if entry is not None:\n+            if entry.get(\"kind\") in (\"permanent\", \"false_positive\"):\n+                need_reopen.append(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+    return count, need_reopen\n+\n+\n+def resurface_stale_skips(\n+    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int\n+) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    resurfaced: list[str] = []\n+    for fid in list(skipped):\n+        entry = skipped[fid]\n+        if entry.get(\"kind\") != \"temporary\":\n+            continue\n+        review_after = entry.get(\"review_after\")\n+        if review_after is None:\n+            continue\n+        skipped_at = entry.get(\"skipped_at_scan\", 0)\n+        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            resurfaced.append(fid)\n+    return resurfaced\n+\n*** End Patch\n"

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_meta.py\n+\"\"\"Execution log and metadata operations.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import ExecutionLogEntry, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults\n+from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now\n+\n+\n+_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000\n+\n+\n+def _get_log_cap() -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited.\"\"\"\n+    try:\n+        from desloppify.core.config import load_config\n+\n+        config = load_config()\n+        value = config.get(\"execution_log_max_entries\", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)\n+        return max(0, int(value))\n+    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\n+        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES\n+\n+\n+def append_log_entry(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    action: str,\n+    *,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,\n+    cluster_name: str | None = None,\n+    actor: str = \"user\",\n+    note: str | None = None,\n+    detail: dict | None = None,\n+) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log.\"\"\"\n+    log = plan.get(\"execution_log\", [])\n+    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {\n+        \"timestamp\": utc_now(),\n+        \"action\": action,\n+        \"issue_ids\": issue_ids or [],\n+        \"cluster_name\": cluster_name,\n+        \"actor\": actor,\n+        \"note\": note,\n+        \"detail\": detail or {},\n+    }\n+    log.append(entry)\n+    cap = _get_log_cap()\n+    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:\n+        plan[\"execution_log\"] = log[-cap:]\n+\n+\n+def describe_issue(\n+    plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str, description: str | None\n+) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Set or clear an augmented description on an issue.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    overrides = plan[\"overrides\"]\n+    if issue_id not in overrides:\n+        overrides[issue_id] = {\"issue_id\": issue_id, \"created_at\": now}\n+    overrides[issue_id][\"description\"] = description\n+    overrides[issue_id][\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+\n+def annotate_issue(\n+    plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str, note: str | None\n+) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Set or clear a note on an issue.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    overrides = plan[\"overrides\"]\n+    if issue_id not in overrides:\n+        overrides[issue_id] = {\"issue_id\": issue_id, \"created_at\": now}\n+    overrides[issue_id][\"note\"] = note\n+    overrides[issue_id][\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n*** End Patch\n"
}

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_lifecycle.py\n+\"\"\"Plan lifecycle operations.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (\n+    PlanModel,\n+    SkipEntry,\n+    empty_plan,\n+    ensure_plan_defaults,\n+)\n+from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now\n+\n+\n+def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Set the active cluster focus.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    if cluster_name not in plan[\"clusters\"]:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+    plan[\"active_cluster\"] = cluster_name\n+\n+\n+def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Clear the active cluster focus.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    plan[\"active_cluster\"] = None\n+\n+\n+def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp.\"\"\"\n+    created = plan.get(\"created\", utc_now())\n+    plan.clear()\n+    for k, v in empty_plan().items():\n+        plan[k] = v\n+    plan[\"created\"] = created\n+    # Sentinel so the next scan seeds real start scores after reset.\n+    plan[\"plan_start_scores\"] = {\"reset\": True}\n+\n+\n+def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Remove issue IDs from queue, skipped set, and clusters.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    found = 0\n+\n+    purge_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        was_present = False\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+            was_present = True\n+        if fid in skipped:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            was_present = True\n+        for cluster in plan.get(\"clusters\", {}).values():\n+            ids = cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", [])\n+            if fid in ids:\n+                ids.remove(fid)\n+                was_present = True\n+        override = plan.get(\"overrides\", {}).get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\"):\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = utc_now()\n+        if was_present:\n+            found += 1\n+\n+    return found\n+\n*** End Patch\n"
}

flat_dir_issues = _build_flat_dir_issues(by_detector)\n    if flat_dir_issues:\n        evidence[\"flat_dir_issues\"] = flat_dir_issues\n\n    large_dist = _build_large_file_distribution(by_detector)\n    if large_dist:\n        evidence[\"large_file_distribution\"] = large_dist\n\n    security_hotspots = _build_security_hotspots(by_detector)\n    if security_hotspots:\n        evidence[\"security_hotspots\"] = security_hotspots\n\n    signal_density = _build_signal_density(by_file)\n    if signal_density:\n        evidence[\"signal_density\"] = signal_density\n\n    systemic = _build_systemic_patterns(smell_counter, smell_files)\n    if systemic:\n        evidence[\"systemic_patterns\"] = systemic\n\n    return evidence\n\n\n__all__ = [\"gather_mechanical_evidence\"]\nPY"
}

{len(si.resolved_since_last)}\")\n        print(colorize(\"\\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:\", \"dim\"))\n        print()\n        print(prompt)\n        return\n\n    _cmd_triage_dashboard(args)\n\n\ncmd_stage_observe = _cmd_stage_observe\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN\",\n    \"_triage_coverage\",\n    \"_validate_attestation\",\n    \"cmd_plan_triage\",\n    \"cmd_stage_observe\",\n]\nPY"
}

field\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults, triage_clusters\nfrom desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TriageInput:\n    \"\"\"All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics.\"\"\"\n\n    open_issues: dict[str, dict]\n    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]\n    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]\n    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]\n    new_since_last: set[str]\n    resolved_since_last: set[str]\n    previously_dismissed: list[str]\n    triage_version: int\n    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]\n    completed_clusters: list[dict]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass DismissedIssue:\n    \"\"\"An issue the LLM says doesn't make sense.\"\"\"\n\n    issue_id: str\n    reason: str\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ContradictionNote:\n    \"\"\"Record of a resolved contradiction.\"\"\"\n\n    kept: str\n    dismissed: str\n    reason: str\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TriageResult:\n    \"\"\"Parsed and validated LLM triage output.\"\"\"\n\n    strategy_summary: str\n    epics: list[dict]\n    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = field(default_factory=list)\n    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)\n    priority_rationale: str = \"\"\n\n\ndef collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:\n    \"\"\"Gather all data needed for the triage LLM prompt.\"\"\"\n    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n    issues = state.get(\"issues\", {})\n    meta = plan.get(\"epic_triage_meta\", {})\n    epics = triage_clusters(plan)\n\n    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}\n    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}\n    for issue_id, issue in issues.items():\n        if issue.get(\"status\") != \"open\":\n            continue\n        if issue.get(\"detector\") in (\"review\", \"concerns\"):\n            open_review[issue_id] = issue\n        else:\n            open_mechanical[issue_id] = issue\n\n    triaged_ids = set(meta.get(\"triaged_ids\", []))\n    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())\n    new_since = current_review_ids - triaged_ids\n    resolved_since = triaged_ids - current_review_ids\n    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get(\"dismissed_ids\", []))\n    version = int(meta.get(\"version\", 0)) + 1\n\n    resolved_issue_objs = {\n        issue_id: issues[issue_id] for issue_id in resolved_since if issue_id in issues\n    }\n\n    last_completed = meta.get(\"last_completed_at\", \"\")\n    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get(\"completed_clusters\", [])\n    if last_completed:\n        recent_completed = [\n            cluster\n            for cluster in all_completed\n            if cluster.get(\"completed_at\", \"\") > last_completed\n        ]\n    else:\n        recent_completed = list(all_completed)\n\n    return TriageInput(\n        open_issues=open_review,\n        mechanical_issues=open_mechanical,\n        existing_epics=dict(epics),\n        dimension_scores=state.get(\"dimension_scores\", {}),\n        new_since_last=new_since,\n        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,\n        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,\n        triage_version=version,\n        resolved_issues=resolved_issue_objs,\n        completed_clusters=recent_completed,\n    )\n\n\n_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = \"\"\"\\\nYou are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce\na coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review issues.\n\nYour plan should:\n- Cluster issues by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector\n- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence\n- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense\n- Dismiss issues that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives\n- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment\n- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan\n- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all issues coherently\n\nAvailable directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.\n\nAvailable plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):\n- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order\n- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic\n- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note \"why\" --attest \"...\"` — permanently dismiss\n- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note \"revisit later\"` — temporarily defer\n- `desloppify plan resolve <id> --note \"what I did\" --attest \"...\"` — mark resolved\n- `desloppify plan reorder <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder\n- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster\n- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress\n- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review issues\n\nYour output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Issues not assigned to any epic\nwill remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed issues will be\nremoved from the queue with your stated reason.\n\nRespond with a single JSON object matching this schema:\n{\n  \"strategy_summary\": \"2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state\",\n  \"epics\": [\n    {\n      \"name\": \"slug-name\",\n      \"thesis\": \"imperative one-liner\",\n      \"direction\": \"delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline\",\n      \"root_cause\": \"why this cluster exists\",\n      \"issue_ids\": [\"id1\", \"id2\"],\n      \"dismissed\": [\"id3\"],\n      \"agent_safe\": true,\n      \"dependency_order\": 1,\n      \"action_steps\": [\"step 1\", \"step 2\"],\n      \"status\": \"pending\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"dismissed_issues\": [\n    {\"issue_id\": \"id\", \"reason\": \"why this issue doesn't make sense\"}\n  ],\n  \"contradiction_notes\": [\n    {\"kept\": \"issue_id\", \"dismissed\": \"issue_id\", \"reason\": \"why\"}\n  ],\n  \"priority_rationale\": \"why the dependency_order is what it is\"\n}\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef build_triage_prompt(si: TriageInput) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Build the user-facing prompt content with all issue data.\"\"\"\n    parts: list[str] = []\n\n    if si.existing_epics:\n        parts.append(\"## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)\")\n        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):\n            status = epic.get(\"status\", \"pending\")\n            thesis = epic.get(\"thesis\", \"\")\n            direction = epic.get(\"direction\", \"\")\n            issue_ids = epic.get(\"issue_ids\", [])\n            parts.append(\n                f\"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}\"\n                f\"\\n  Issues: {', '.join(issue_ids[:10])}\"\n                f\"{'...' if len(issue_ids) > 10 else ''}\"\n            )\n        parts.append(\"\")\n\n    if si.new_since_last:\n        parts.append(f\"## New issues since last triage ({len(si.new_since_last)})\")\n        for issue_id in sorted(si.new_since_last):\n            issue = si.open_issues.get(issue_id, {})\n            parts.append(f\"- {issue_id}: {issue.get('summary', '(no summary)')}\")\n        parts.append(\"\")\n\n    if si.resolved_since_last:\n        parts.append(f\"## Resolved since last triage ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})\")\n        for issue_id in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):\n            parts.append(f\"- {issue_id}\")\n        parts.append(\"\")\n\n    parts.append(f\"## All open review issues ({len(si.open_issues)})\")\n    for issue_id, issue in sorted(si.open_issues.items()):\n        detail = issue.get(\"detail\", {}) if isinstance(issue.get(\"detail\"), dict) else {}\n        suggestion = detail.get(\"suggestion\", \"\")\n        dimension = detail.get(\"dimension\", \"\")\n        confidence = issue.get(\"confidence\", \"medium\")\n        file_path = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n        summary = issue.get(\"summary\", \"\")\n        parts.append(f\"- [{confidence}] {issue_id}\")\n        parts.append(f\"  File: {file_path}\")\n        if dimension:\n            parts.append(f\"  Dimension: {dimension}\")\n        parts.append(f\"  Summary: {summary}\")\n        if suggestion:\n            parts.append(f\"  Suggestion: {suggestion}\")\n    parts.append(\"\")\n\n    if si.dimension_scores:\n        parts.append(\"## Dimension scores (context)\")\n        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):\n            if isinstance(data, dict):\n                score = data.get(\"score\", \"?\")\n                strict = data.get(\"strict\", score)\n                issues = data.get(\"failing\", 0)\n                parts.append(f\"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)\")\n        parts.append(\"\")\n\n    if si.previously_dismissed:\n        parts.append(f\"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})\")\n        parts.append(\"Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.\")\n        for issue_id in si.previously_dismissed:\n            parts.append(f\"- {issue_id}\")\n        parts.append(\"\")\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(parts)\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"ContradictionNote\",\n    \"DismissedIssue\",\n    \"TriageInput\",\n    \"TriageResult\",\n    \"_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT\",\n    \"build_triage_prompt\",\n    \"collect_triage_input\",\n]\nPY"

desloppify.engine._plan.schema import VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS\n\nfrom .epic_triage_prompt import ContradictionNote, DismissedIssue, TriageResult\n\n\nISSUE_ID_RE = re.compile(r\"[a-z_]+::[a-f0-9]{8,}\")\n\n\ndef extract_issue_citations(text: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> set[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract issue IDs cited in free text.\"\"\"\n    cited: set[str] = set()\n    for match in ISSUE_ID_RE.finditer(text):\n        candidate = match.group()\n        if candidate in valid_ids:\n            cited.add(candidate)\n    for token in re.findall(r\"[0-9a-f]{8,}\", text):\n        for valid_id in valid_ids:\n            if valid_id.endswith(\"::\" + token):\n                cited.add(valid_id)\n                break\n    return cited\n\n\ndef parse_triage_result(raw: dict, valid_ids: set[str]) -> TriageResult:\n    \"\"\"Parse and validate raw LLM output into a TriageResult.\"\"\"\n    strategy_summary = str(raw.get(\"strategy_summary\", \"\"))\n\n    epics: list[dict] = []\n    for raw_epic in raw.get(\"epics\", []):\n        if not isinstance(raw_epic, dict):\n            continue\n        name = str(raw_epic.get(\"name\", \"\")).strip()\n        if not name:\n            continue\n        direction = str(raw_epic.get(\"direction\", \"simplify\")).strip()\n        if direction not in VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS:\n            direction = \"simplify\"\n\n        issue_ids = [\n            issue_id\n            for issue_id in raw_epic.get(\"issue_ids\", [])\n            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id in valid_ids\n        ]\n        dismissed = [\n            issue_id\n            for issue_id in raw_epic.get(\"dismissed\", [])\n            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id in valid_ids\n        ]\n        action_steps = [\n            str(step)\n            for step in raw_epic.get(\"action_steps\", [])\n            if isinstance(step, str)\n        ]\n\n        epics.append(\n            {\n                \"name\": name,\n                \"thesis\": str(raw_epic.get(\"thesis\", \"\")),\n                \"direction\": direction,\n                \"root_cause\": str(raw_epic.get(\"root_cause\", \"\")),\n                \"issue_ids\": issue_ids,\n                \"dismissed\": dismissed,\n                \"agent_safe\": bool(raw_epic.get(\"agent_safe\", False)),\n                \"dependency_order\": int(raw_epic.get(\"dependency_order\", 999)),\n                \"action_steps\": action_steps,\n                \"status\": str(raw_epic.get(\"status\", \"pending\")),\n            }\n        )\n\n    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = []\n    for dismissed in raw.get(\"dismissed_issues\", []):\n        if not isinstance(dismissed, dict):\n            continue\n        issue_id = str(dismissed.get(\"issue_id\", \"\"))\n        if issue_id in valid_ids:\n            dismissed_issues.append(\n                DismissedIssue(issue_id=issue_id, reason=str(dismissed.get(\"reason\", \"\")))\n            )\n\n    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = []\n    for note in raw.get(\"contradiction_notes\", []):\n        if not isinstance(note, dict):\n            continue\n        contradiction_notes.append(\n            ContradictionNote(\n                kept=str(note.get(\"kept\", \"\")),\n                dismissed=str(note.get(\"dismissed\", \"\")),\n                reason=str(note.get(\"reason\", \"\")),\n            )\n        )\n\n    priority_rationale = str(raw.get(\"priority_rationale\", \"\"))\n\n    return TriageResult(\n        strategy_summary=strategy_summary,\n        epics=epics,\n        dismissed_issues=dismissed_issues,\n        contradiction_notes=contradiction_notes,\n        priority_rationale=priority_rationale,\n    )\n\n\n__all__ = [\"ISSUE_ID_RE\", \"extract_issue_citations\", \"parse_triage_result\"]\nPY"
}

collect_triage_input,\n)\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Timestamp of most recent genuine review import.\"\"\"\n    real_modes = {\"holistic\", \"per_file\", \"trusted_internal\", \"attested_external\"}\n    audit = state.get(\"assessment_import_audit\", [])\n    if isinstance(audit, list):\n        for entry in reversed(audit):\n            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get(\"mode\") in real_modes:\n                timestamp = entry.get(\"timestamp\")\n                if timestamp:\n                    return str(timestamp)\n    holistic = (state.get(\"review_cache\") or {}).get(\"holistic\")\n    if isinstance(holistic, dict):\n        return holistic.get(\"reviewed_at\")\n    return None\n\n\ndef detect_recurring_patterns(\n    open_issues: dict[str, dict],\n    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],\n) -> dict[str, dict]:\n    \"\"\"Detect dimensions with both resolved and current open issues.\"\"\"\n\n    def _dimension(issue: dict) -> str:\n        detail = issue.get(\"detail\", {})\n        if isinstance(detail, dict):\n            return detail.get(\"dimension\", \"\")\n        return \"\"\n\n    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}\n    for issue_id, issue in open_issues.items():\n        dim = _dimension(issue)\n        if dim:\n            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(issue_id)\n\n    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}\n    for issue_id, issue in resolved_issues.items():\n        dim = _dimension(issue)\n        if dim:\n            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(issue_id)\n\n    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}\n    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):\n        recurring[dim] = {\n            \"open\": open_by_dim[dim],\n            \"resolved\": resolved_by_dim[dim],\n        }\n    return recurring\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TriageDeps:\n    \"\"\"Injectable dependencies for the triage engine.\"\"\"\n\n    llm_call: Any = None\n\n\ndef triage_epics(\n    plan: PlanModel,\n    state: StateModel,\n    *,\n    deps: TriageDeps | None = None,\n    dry_run: bool = False,\n    trigger: state)\n\n    prompt = build_triage_prompt(triage_input)\n    valid_ids = set(triage_input.open_issues.keys())\n\n    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:\n        result = TriageMutationResult(dry_run=True)\n        result.strategy_summary = f\"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(triage_input.open_issues)} issues\"\n        return result\n\n    try:\n        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)\n        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)\n    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:\n        logger.error(\"Epic triage LLM response parse error: %s\", exc)\n        result = TriageMutationResult()\n        result.strategy_summary = f\"Triage failed: {exc}\"\n        return result\n\n    triage = parse_triage_result(raw_json, valid_ids)\n    return apply_triage_to_plan(plan, state, triage, trigger=trigger)\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"ContradictionNote\",\n    \"DismissedIssue\",\n    \"ISSUE_ID_RE\",\n    \"TriageDeps\",\n    \"TriageInput\",\n    \"TriageMutationResult\",\n    \"TriageResult\",\n    \"apply_triage_to_plan\",\n    \"build_triage_prompt\",\n all issue data.\"\"\"\n+    parts: list[str] = []\n+\n+    if si.existing_epics:\n+        parts.append(\"## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)\")\n+        for name, epic in            issue_ids = epic.get(\"issue_ids\", [])\n+            parts.append(\n+                f\"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}\"\n+                f\"\\n  Issues: {', '.join(issue_ids[:10])}\"\n+                f\"{'...' if len(issue_ids) > 10 else ''}\"\n+            )\n+        parts.append(\"\")\n+\n+    if si.new_since_last:\n+        parts.append(f\"## New issues since last triage ({len(si.new_since_last)})\")\n+        for issue_id in sorted(si.new_since_last):\n+            issue = si.open_issues.get(issue_id, {})\n+            parts.append(f\"- {issue_id}: {issue.get('summary', '(no summary)')}\")\n+        parts.append(\"\")\n+\n+    if si.resolved_since_last:\n+        parts.append(f\"## Resolved since last triage ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})\")\n+        for issue_id in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):\n+            parts.append(f\"- {issue_id}\")\n+        parts.append(\"\")\n+\n+    parts.append(f\"## All open review issues ({len(si.open_issues)})\")\n+    for issue_id, issue in sorted(si.open_issues.items()):\n+        detail = issue.get(\"detail\", {}) if isinstance(issue.get(\"detail\"), dict) else {}\n+        suggestion = detail.get(\"suggestion\", \"\")\n+        dimension = detail.get(\"dimension\", \"\")\n+        confidence = issue.get(\"confidence\", \"medium\")\n+        file_path = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        summary = issue.get(\"summary\", \"\")\n+        parts.append(f\"- [{confidence}] {issue_id}\")\n+        parts.append(f\"  File: {file_path}\")\n+        if dimension:\n+            parts.append(f\"  Dimension: {dimension}\")\n+        parts.append(f\"  Summary: {summary}\")\n+        if suggestion:\n+            parts.append(f\"  Suggestion: {suggestion}\")\n+    parts.append(\"\")\n+\n+    if si.dimension_scores:\n+        parts.append(\"## Dimension scores (context)\")\n+        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):\n+            if isinstance(data, dict):\n+                score = data.get(\"score\", \"?\")\n+                strict = data.get(\"strict\", score)\n+                issues = data.get(\"failing\", 0)\n+                parts.append(f\"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)\")\n+        parts.append(\"\")\n+\n+    if si.previously_dismissed:\n+        parts.append(f\"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})\")\n+        parts.append(\"Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.\")\n+        for issue_id in si.previously_dismissed:\n+            parts.append(f\"- {issue_id}\")\n+        parts.append(\"\")\n+\n+    return \"\\n\".join(parts)\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\n+    \"ContradictionNote\",\n+    \"DismissedIssue\",\n+    \"TriageInput\",\n+    \"TriageResult\",\n+    \"_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT\",\n+    \"build_triage_prompt\",\n+    \"collect_triage_input\",\n+]\n+\n*** End Patch\n"

cited in free text.\"\"\"\n+    cited: set[str] = set()\n+    for match in ISSUE_ID_RE.finditer(text):\n+        candidate = match.group()\n+        if candidate in valid_ids:\n+            cited.add(candidate)\n+    for token in re.findall(r\"[0-9a-f]{8,}\", text):\n+        for valid_id in valid_ids:\n+            if valid_id.endswith(\"::\" + token):\n+                cited.add(valid_id)\n+                break\n+    return cited\n+\n+\n+def parse_triage_result(raw: dict, valid_ids: set[str]) -> TriageResult:\n+    \"\"\"Parse and validate raw LLM output into a TriageResult.\"\"\"\n+    strategy_summary = str(raw.get(\"strategy_summary\", \"\"))\n+\n+    epics: list[dict] = []\n+    for raw_epic in raw.get(\"epics\", []):\n+        if not isinstance(raw_epic, dict):\n+            continue\n+        name = str(raw_epic.get(\"name\", \"\")).strip()\n+        if not name:\n+            continue\n+        direction = str(raw_epic.get(\"direction\", \"simplify\")).strip()\n+        if direction not in VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS:\n+            direction = \"simplify\"\n+\n+        issue_ids = [\n+            issue_id\n+ for issue_id in raw_epic.get(\"dismissed\", [])\n+            if isinstance(issue_id, str) and issue_id in valid_ids\n+        ]\n+        action_steps = [\n+            str(step)\n+            for step in raw_epic.get(\"action_steps\", [])\n+            if isinstance(step, str)\n+        ]\n+\n+        epics.append(\n+            {\n+                \"name\": name,\n+                \"thesis\": str(raw_epic.get(\"thesis\", \"\")),\n+                \"direction\": direction,\n+                \"root_cause\": str(raw_epic.get(\"root_cause\", \"\")),\n+                \"issue_ids\": issue_ids,\n+                \"dismissed\": dismissed,\n+                \"agent_safe\": bool(raw_epic.get(\"agent_safe\", False)),\n+                \"dependency_order\": int(raw_epic.get(\"dependency_order\", 999)),\n+                \"action_steps\": action_steps,\n+                \"status\": str(raw_epic.get(\"status\", \"pending\")),\n+            }\n+        )\n+\n+    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = []\n+    for dismissed in raw.get(\"dismissed_issues\", []):\n+        if not isinstance(dismissed, dict):\n+            continue\n+        issue_id = str(dismissed.get(\"issue_id\", \"\"))\n+        if issue_id in valid_ids:\n+            dismissed_issues.append(\n+                DismissedIssue(issue_id=issue_id, reason=str(dismissed.get(\"reason\", \"\")))\n+            )\n+\n+    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = []\n+    for note in raw.get(\"contradiction_notes\", []):\n+        if not isinstance(note, dict):\n+            continue\n+        contradiction_notes.append(\n+            ContradictionNote(\n+                kept=str(note.get(\"kept\", \"\")),\n+                dismissed=str(note.get(\"dismissed\", \"\")),\n+                reason=str(note.get(\"reason\", \"\")),\n+            )\n+        )\n+\n+    priority_rationale = str(raw.get(\"priority_rationale\", \"\"))\n+\n+    return TriageResult(\n+        strategy_summary=strategy_summary,\n+        epics=epics,\n+        dismissed_issues=dismissed_issues,\n+        contradiction_notes=contradiction_notes,\n+        priority_rationale=priority_rationale,\n+    )\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"ISSUE_ID_RE\", \"extract_issue_citations\", \"parse_triage_result\"]\n+\n*** End min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)\n+\n+\n+def move_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    issue_ids: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+\n+    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered.\n+    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS\n+\n+    issue_ids = [issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if issue_id not in TRIAGE_IDS]\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    for issue_id in issue_ids:\n+        skipped.pop(issue_id, None)\n+\n+    for issue_id in issue_ids:\n+        if issue_id in order:\n+            order.remove(issue_id)\n+\n+    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)\n+\n+    for i, issue_id in enumerate(issue_ids):\n+        order.insert(idx + i, issue_id)\n+\n+    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+        existing_promoted = set(promoted)\n+        for issue_id in issue_ids:\n+            if issue_id not in existing_promoted:\n+                promoted.append(issue_id)\n+                existing_promoted.add(issue_id)\n+        plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = promoted\n+\n+    return len(issue_ids)\n+\n*** End Patch\n"
}

return count\n+\n+\n+def unskip_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.\"\"\"\n+   "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_lifecycle.py\n+\"\"\"Plan lifecycle operations.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (\n+    PlanModel,\n+    SkipEntry,\n+    empty_plan,\n+    ensure_plan_defaults,\n+)\n+from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now\n+\n+\n+def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Set the active cluster focus.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    if cluster_name not in plan[\"clusters\"]:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+    plan[\"active_cluster\"] = cluster_name\n+\n+\n+def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Clear the active cluster focus.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    plan[\"active_cluster\"] = None\n+\n+\n+def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp.\"\"\"\n+    created = plan.get(\"created\", utc_now())\n+    plan.clear()\n+    for key, value in empty_plan().items():\n+        plan[key] = value\n+    plan[\"created\"] = created\n+    # Sentinel so the next scan seeds real start scores after reset.\n+    plan[\"plan_start_scores\"] = {\"reset\": True}\n+\n+\n+def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Remove issue IDs from queue, skipped set, and clusters.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    found = 0\n+\n+    purge_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for issue_id in issue_ids:\n+        was_present = False\n+        if issue_id in order:\n+            order.remove(issue_id)\n+            was_present = True\n+        if issue_id in skipped:\n+            skipped.pop(issue_id)\n+            was_present = True\n+        for cluster in plan.get(\"clusters\", {}).values():\n+            ids = cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", [])\n+            if issue_id in ids:\n+                ids.remove(issue_id)\n+                was_present = True\n+        override = plan.get(\"overrides\", {}).get(issue_id)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\"):\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = utc_now()\n+        if was_present:\n+            found += 1\n+\n+    return found\n+\n*** End Patch\n"
}

entry[\"signals\"].append(f\"{int(max_params)} params\")\n+        max_nesting = _safe_num(signals.get(\"max_nesting\"))\n+        if max_nesting >= 4:\n+            entry[\"signals\"].append(f\"nesting depth {int(max_nesting)}\")\n+        complexity = _safe_num(signals.get(\"complexity_score\"))\n+        entry[\"complexity_score\"] = max(entry[\"complexity_score\"], complexity)\n+\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"smells\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        smell_id = _get_detail(issue, \"smell_id\", \"\")\n+        if filepath in file_data:\n+            if smell_id == \"monster_function\":\n+                file_data[filepath][\"monster_functions\"] += 1\n+            elif smell_id in (\"cyclomatic_complexity\", \"high_cyclomatic\"):\n+                file_data[filepath][\"cyclomatic_hotspots\"] += 1\n+\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"responsibility_cohesion\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        if filepath in file_data:\n+            clusters = _safe_num(_get_detail(issue, \"cluster_count\"))\n+            file_data[filepath][\"component_count\"] = max(\n+                file_data[filepath][\"component_count\"], clusters\n+            )\n+\n+    for entry in file_data.values():\n+        entry[\"_score\"] = (\n+            entry[\"loc\"] / 100\n+            + entry[\"complexity_score\"]\n+            + entry[\"component_count\"] * 3\n+            + entry[\"monster_functions\"] * 10\n+        )\n+        entry[\"signals\"] = list(dict.fromkeys(entry[\"signals\"]))\n+\n+    ranked = sorted(file_data.values(), key=lambda e: -e[\"_score\"])[:20]\n+    for entry in ranked:\n+        del entry[\"_score\"]\n+    return ranked\n+\n*** End Patch\n"
}

{
  "cmd": "cat > desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage_handlers.py <<'PY'\n\"\"\"Handler for ``plan triage`` subcommand.\n\nImplements a staged workflow: OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT\nwith a fast-track CONFIRM-EXISTING skip path.\n\nStage gates validate **plan data enrichment**, not text reports:\n- OBSERVE: lightweight — write an analysis of themes/root causes (100 char min)\n- REFLECT: compare current issues against completed work (recurring patterns)\n- ORGANIZE: structural — each manual cluster must have description + action_steps\n- COMMIT: strategy must be substantive (200 char min) or \"same\"\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\n\nfrom desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_issue_id\nfrom desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime\nfrom desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan\nfrom desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import confirmations as triage_confirmations\nfrom desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import display as triage_display\nfrom desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import helpers as triage_helpers\nfrom desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import stages as triage_stages\nfrom desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE\nfrom desloppify.core.output_api import colorize\nfrom desloppify.engine.plan import (\n    TRIAGE_IDS,\n    append_log_entry,\n    build_triage_prompt,\n    collect_triage_input,\n    detect_recurring_patterns,\n    extract_issue_citations,\n    load_plan,\n    purge_ids,\n    review_issue_snapshot_hash,\n    save_plan,\n)\nfrom desloppify.state import utc_now\n\n\ndef _sync_triage_module_dependencies() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Mirror patchable triage handler globals into split sub-modules.\"\"\"\n    # helpers.py\n    triage_helpers.command_runtime = command_runtime\n    triage_helpers.append_log_entry = append_log_entry\n    triage_helpers.purge_ids = purge_ids\n    triage_helpers.review_issue_snapshot_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash\n    triage_helpers.save_plan = save_plan\n    triage_helpers.utc_now = utc_now\n    triage_helpers.colorize = colorize\n\n    # display.py\n    triage_display.command_runtime = command_runtime\n    triage_display.collect_triage_input = collect_triage_input\n    triage_display.detect_recurring_patterns = detect_recurring_patterns\n    triage_display.load_plan = load_plan\n    triage_display.short_issue_id = short_issue_id\n    triage_display.colorize = colorize\n\n    # stages.py\n    triage_stages.command_runtime = command_runtime\n    triage_stages.append_log_entry = append_log_entry\n    triage_stages.collect_triage_input = collect_triage_input\n    triage_stages.detect_recurring_patterns = detect_recurring_patterns\n    triage_stages.extract_issue_citations = extract_issue_citations\n    triage_stages.load_plan = load_plan\n    triage_stages.save_plan = save_plan\n    triage_stages.utc_now = utc_now\n\n    # confirmations.py\n    triage_confirmations.command_runtime = command_runtime\n    triage_confirmations.append_log_entry = append_log_entry\n    triage_confirmations.collect_triage_input = collect_triage_input\n    triage_confirmations.detect_recurring_patterns = detect_recurring_patterns\n    triage_confirmations.load_plan = load_plan\n    triage_confirmations.save_plan = save_plan\n    triage_confirmations.utc_now = utc_now\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Start (manual trigger)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef _cmd_triage_start(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Manually inject triage stage IDs into the queue and clear prior stages.\"\"\"\n    _sync_triage_module_dependencies()\n    plan = load_plan()\n\n    if triage_helpers._has_triage_in_queue(plan):\n        print(colorize(\"  Planning mode stages are already in the queue.\", \"yellow\"))\n        meta = plan.get(\"epic_triage_meta\", {})\n        stages = meta.get(\"triage_stages\", {})\n        if stages:\n            print(colorize(f\"  {len(stages)} stage(s) in progress — clearing to restart.\", \"yellow\"))\n            meta[\"triage_stages\"] = {}\n            triage_helpers._inject_triage_stages(plan)\n            save_plan(plan)\n            append_log_entry(\n                plan,\n                \"triage_start\",\n                actor=\"user\",\n                detail={\"action\": \"restart\", \"cleared_stages\": list(stages.keys())},\n            )\n            save_plan(plan)\n            print(colorize(\"  Stages cleared. Begin with observe:\", \"green\"))\n        else:\n            print(colorize(\"  Begin with observe:\", \"green\"))\n        print(colorize(f\"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}\", \"dim\"))\n        return\n\n    # Inject all 4 stage IDs\n    triage_helpers._inject_triage_stages(plan)\n    meta = plan.setdefault(\"epic_triage_meta\", {})\n    meta[\"triage_stages\"] = {}\n    save_plan(plan)\n\n    append_log_entry(plan, \"triage_start\", actor=\"user\", detail={\"action\": \"start\"})\n    save_plan(plan)\n\n    runtime = command_runtime(args)\n    si = collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)\n    print(colorize(\"  Planning mode started (4 stages queued).\", \"green\"))\n    print(f\"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}\")\n    print(colorize(\"  Begin with observe:\", \"dim\"))\n    print(colorize(f\"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}\", \"dim\"))\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Router\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef cmd_plan_triage(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Run epic triage: staged workflow OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT.\"\"\"\n    _sync_triage_module_dependencies()\n\n    runtime = command_runtime(args)\n    state = runtime.state\n    if not require_completed_scan(state):\n        return\n\n    # Route: --start\n    if getattr(args, \"start\", False):\n        _cmd_triage_start(args)\n        return\n\n    # Route: --confirm observe/reflect/organize\n    if getattr(args, \"confirm\", None):\n        triage_confirmations._cmd_confirm_stage(args)\n        return\n\n    # Route: --complete\n    if getattr(args, \"complete\", False):\n        triage_stages._cmd_triage_complete(args)\n        return\n\n    # Route: --confirm-existing\n    if getattr(args, \"confirm_existing\", False):\n        triage_stages._cmd_confirm_existing(args)\n        return\n\n    # Route: --stage observe/reflect/organize\n    stage = getattr(args, \"stage\", None)\n    if stage == \"observe\":\n        triage_stages._cmd_stage_observe(args)\n        return\n    if stage == \"reflect\":\n        triage_stages._cmd_stage_reflect(args)\n        return\n    if stage == \"organize\":\n        triage_stages._cmd_stage_organize(args)\n        return\n\n    # Dry-run mode\n    if getattr(args, \"dry_run\", False):\n        plan = load_plan()\n        si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)\n        prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)\n        print(colorize(\"  Epic triage — dry run\", \"bold\"))\n        print(colorize(\"  \" + \"─\" * 60, \"dim\"))\n        print(f\"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}\")\n        print(f\"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}\")\n        print(f\"  New since last: {len(si.new_since_last)}\")\n        print(f\"  Resolved since last: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}\")\n        print(colorize(\"\\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:\", \"dim\"))\n        print()\n        print(prompt)\n        return\n\n    # Default: dashboard\n    triage_display._cmd_triage_dashboard(args)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Compatibility exports\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _triage_coverage(\n    plan: dict,\n    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,\n) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:\n    _sync_triage_module_dependencies()\n    return triage_helpers._triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=open_review_ids)\n\n\n_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = triage_confirmations._MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN\n\n\ndef _validate_attestation(\n    attestation: str,\n    stage: str,\n    *,\n    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,\n    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,\n) -> str | None:\n    return triage_confirmations._validate_attestation(\n        attestation,\n        stage,\n        dimensions=dimensions,\n        cluster_names=cluster_names,\n    )\n\n\ndef cmd_stage_observe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:\n    _sync_triage_module_dependencies()\n    triage_stages._cmd_stage_observe(args)\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"cmd_plan_triage\",\n    \"cmd_stage_observe\",\n    \"_triage_coverage\",\n    \"_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN\",\n    \"_validate_attestation\",\n]\nPY",

}

positives,\nresolves contradictions, and orders by dependency.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults\nfrom desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel\n\nfrom .epic_triage_apply import TriageMutationResult, apply_triage_to_plan\nfrom .epic_triage_parsing import ISSUE_ID_RE, extract_issue_citations, parse_triage_result\nfrom .epic_triage_prompt import (\n    _TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n    ContradictionNote,\n    DismissedIssue,\n    TriageInput,\n    TriageResult,\n    build_triage_prompt,\n    collect_triage_input,\n)\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Completed-work helpers\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset).\"\"\"\n    REAL_MODES = {\"holistic\", \"per_file\", \"trusted_internal\", \"attested_external\"}\n    audit = state.get(\"assessment_import_audit\", [])\n    if isinstance(audit, list):\n        for entry in reversed(audit):\n            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get(\"mode\") in REAL_MODES:\n                ts = entry.get(\"timestamp\")\n                if ts:\n                    return str(ts)\n    holistic = (state.get(\"review_cache\") or {}).get(\"holistic\")\n    if isinstance(holistic, dict):\n        return holistic.get(\"reviewed_at\")\n    return None\n\n\ndef detect_recurring_patterns(\n    open_issues: dict[str, dict],\n    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],\n) -> dict[str, dict]:\n    \"\"\"Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open issues.\n\n    Returns ``{dimension: {\"open\": [ids], \"resolved\": [ids]}}``.\n    A dimension with both resolved and open issues signals a potential\n    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def _dimension(issue: dict) -> str:\n        detail = issue.get(\"detail\", {})\n        if isinstance(detail, dict):\n            return detail.get(\"dimension\", \"\")\n        return \"\"\n\n    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}\n    for issue_id, issue in open_issues.items():\n        dim = _dimension(issue)\n        if dim:\n            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(issue_id)\n\n    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}\n    for issue_id, issue in resolved_issues.items():\n        dim = _dimension(issue)\n        if dim:\n            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(issue_id)\n\n    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}\n    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):\n        recurring[dim] = {\n            \"open\": open_by_dim[dim],\n            \"resolved\": resolved_by_dim[dim],\n        }\n    return recurring\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Top-level orchestrator\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n@dataclass\nclass TriageDeps:\n    \"\"\"Injectable dependencies for the triage engine.\"\"\"\n\n    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response\n\n\ndef triage_epics(\n    plan: PlanModel,\n    state: StateModel,\n    *,\n    deps: TriageDeps | None = None,\n    dry_run: bool = False,\n    trigger: str = \"manual\",\n) -> TriageMutationResult:\n    \"\"\"Run epic triage: collect input, call LLM, apply results.\"\"\"\n    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)\n\n    prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)\n    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())\n\n    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:\n        result = TriageMutationResult(dry_run=True)\n        result.strategy_summary = f\"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_issues)} issues\"\n        return result\n\n    try:\n        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)\n        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)\n    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:\n        logger.error(\"Epic triage LLM response parse error: %s\", exc)\n        result = TriageMutationResult()\n        result.strategy_summary = f\"Triage failed: {exc}\"\n        return result\n\n    triage = parse_triage_result(raw_json, valid_ids)\n    return apply_triage_to_plan(plan, state, triage, trigger=trigger)\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"ContradictionNote\",\n    \"DismissedIssue\",\n    \"ISSUE_ID_RE\",\n    \"TriageDeps\",\n    \"TriageInput\",\n    \"TriageMutationResult\",\n    \"TriageResult\",\n    \"apply_triage_to_plan\",\n    \"build_triage_prompt\",\n    \"collect_triage_input\",\n    \"detect_recurring_patterns\",\n    \"extract_issue_citations\",\n    \"last_real_review_timestamp\",\n    \"parse_triage_result\",\n    \"triage_epics\",\n]\nPY",

}

delegation_density = _score_clamped(\n        100 - (avg_delegation_ratio * 80) - (len(delegation_classes) * 5)\n    )\n    avg_facade_ratio = (\n        sum(f[\"re_export_ratio\"] for f in facade_modules[:10]) / len(facade_modules[:10])\n        if facade_modules\n        else 0.0\n    )\n    definition_directness = _score_clamped(\n        100 - (len(facade_modules) * 8) - (avg_facade_ratio * 50)\n    )\n    type_discipline = _score_clamped(\n        100\n        - (len(typed_dict_violation_files) * 6)\n        - (total_typed_dict_violations * 1.5)\n    )\n    return {\n        \"abstraction_leverage\": abstraction_leverage,\n        \"indirection_cost\": indirection_cost,\n        \"interface_honesty\": interface_honesty,\n        \"delegation_density\": delegation_density,\n        \"definition_directness\": definition_directness,\n        \"type_discipline\": type_discipline,\n    }\n\n\ndef _build_abstraction_leverage_context(\n    *,\n    util_files: list,\n    wrappers_by_file: list,\n) -> dict[str, object]:\n    \"\"\"Context slices that explain abstraction leverage score.\"\"\"\n    context: dict[str, object] = {\"util_files\": sorted(util_files, key=lambda item: -item[\"loc\"])[:20]}\n    if wrappers_by_file:\n        context[\"pass_through_wrappers\"] = wrappers_by_file[:20]\n    return context\n\n\ndef _build_indirection_cost_context(\n    *,\n    indirection_hotspots: list,\n    wide_param_bags: list,\n) -> dict[str, object]:\n    \"\"\"Context slices that explain indirection cost score.\"\"\"\n    context: dict[str, object] = {}\n    if indirection_hotspots:\n        context[\"indirection_hotspots\"] = indirection_hotspots[:20]\n    if wide_param_bags:\n        context[\"wide_param_bags\"] = wide_param_bags[:20]\n    return context\n\n\ndef _build_interface_honesty_context(*, one_impl_interfaces: list) -> dict[str, object]:\n    \"\"\"Context slices that explain interface honesty score.\"\"\"\n    if not one_impl_interfaces:\n        return {}\n    return {\"one_impl_interfaces\": one_impl_interfaces[:20]}\n\n\ndef _build_delegation_density_context(*, delegation_classes: list) -> dict[str, object]:\n    \"\"\"Context slices that explain delegation density score.\"\"\"\n    if not delegation_classes:\n        return {}\n    return {\"delegation_heavy_classes\": delegation_classes}\n\n\ndef _build_definition_directness_context(*, facade_modules: list) -> dict[str, object]:\n    \"\"\"Context slices that explain definition directness score.\"\"\"\n    if not facade_modules:\n        return {}\n    return {\"facade_modules\": facade_modules}\n\n\ndef _build_type_discipline_context(*, typed_dict_violations: list) -> dict[str, object]:\n    \"\"\"Context slices that explain type discipline score.\"\"\"\n    if not typed_dict_violations:\n        return {}\n    return {\"typed_dict_violations\": typed_dict_violations}\n\n\ndef _assemble_context(\n    *,\n    util_files: list,\n    wrapper_rate: float,\n    total_wrappers: int,\n    total_function_signatures: int,\n    wrappers_by_file: list,\n    one_impl_interfaces: list,\n sub_axes: dict[str, float],\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Build the final context dict from collected data and sub-axis scores.\"\"\"\n    context: dict[str, object] = {\n        \"summary\": {\n },\n        \"sub_axes\": sub_axes,\n    }\n\n    context.update(\n        _build_abstraction_leverage_context(\n            util_files=util_files,\n            wrappers_by_file=wrappers_by_file,\n        )\n    )\n    context.update(\n        _build_indirection_cost_context(\n            indirection_hotspots=indirection_hotspots,\n            wide_param_bags=wide_param_bags,\n        )\n    )\n    context.update(\n        _build_interface_honesty_context(one_impl_interfaces=one_impl_interfaces)\n    )\n    context.update(\n context.update(\n        _build_type_discipline_context(typed_dict_violations=typed_dict_violations)\n    )\n\n    return context\nPY\n\n# Append unchanged orchestration + stats from the original   "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_queue.py\n+\"\"\"Queue ordering helpers for plan operations.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults\n+\n+\n+def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Remove a issue ID from queue_order and skipped.\"\"\"\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    if issue_id in order:\n+        order.remove(issue_id)\n+    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.\n+\n+    *issue_ids* are the IDs being moved - used to calculate relative\n+    positions when they already exist in the list.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    moving = set(issue_ids or [])\n+\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        return 0\n+    if position == \"bottom\":\n+        return len(order)\n+\n+    if position in {\"before\", \"after\"} and target:\n+        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)\n+\n+    if position in {\"up\", \"down\"} and offset is not None:\n+        return _resolve_offset_position(\n+            order,\n+            moving,\n+            position=position,\n+            offset=offset,\n+            issue_ids=issue_ids,\n+        )\n+\n+    return len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_relative_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str,\n+) -> int:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):\n+        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:\n+            return i if position == \"before\" else i + 1\n+    return 0 if position == \"before\" else len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):\n+        if item_id == target:\n+            return i\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_offset_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    offset: int,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None,\n+) -> int:\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0 if position == \"up\" else len(order)\n+    first_id = issue_ids[0]\n+    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]\n+    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)\n+    if current_idx is None:\n+        if position == \"up\":\n+            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)\n+        return len(clean_order)\n+    if position == \"up\":\n+        return max(0, current_idx - offset)\n+    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)\n+\n+\n+def move_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    issue_ids: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+\n+    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered\n+    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS\n+\n+    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+\n+    # Remove from skipped if present\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+\n+    # Remove from current position in order\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+\n+    # Resolve insertion point\n+    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)\n+\n+    # Insert in original order\n+    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):\n+        order.insert(idx + i, fid)\n+\n+    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.\n+    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+        existing_promoted = set(promoted)\n+        for fid in issue_ids:\n+            if fid not in existing_promoted:\n+                promoted.append(fid)\n+                existing_promoted.add(fid)\n+        plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = promoted\n+\n+    return len(issue_ids)\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"move_items\", \"_remove_id_from_lists\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Returns count skipped.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    count = 0\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    skip_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)\n+        skipped[fid] = {\n+            \"issue_id\": fid,\n+            \"kind\": kind,\n+            \"reason\": reason,\n+            \"note\": note,\n+            \"attestation\": attestation,\n+            \"created_at\": now,\n+            \"review_after\": review_after,\n+            \"skipped_at_scan\": scan_count,\n+        }\n+        count += 1\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def unskip_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.\n+\n+    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``\n+    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive\n+    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    count = 0\n+    need_reopen: list[str] = []\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+        if entry is not None:\n+            if entry.get(\"kind\") in (\"permanent\", \"false_positive\"):\n+                need_reopen.append(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+    return count, need_reopen\n+\n+\n+def resurface_stale_skips(\n+    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int\n+) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.\n+\n+    Returns list of resurfaced issue IDs.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    resurfaced: list[str] = []\n+    for fid in list(skipped):\n+        entry = skipped[fid]\n+        if entry.get(\"kind\") != \"temporary\":\n+            continue\n+        review_after = entry.get(\"review_after\")\n+        if review_after is None:\n+            continue\n+        skipped_at = entry.get(\"skipped_at_scan\", 0)\n+        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            resurfaced.append(fid)\n+    return resurfaced\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"skip_items\", \"unskip_items\", \"resurface_stale_skips\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_cluster.py\n+\"\"\"Cluster mutation helpers for plan operations.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults\n+from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now\n+\n+from .operations_queue import move_items\n+\n+\n+def create_cluster(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    name: str,\n+    description: str | None = None,\n+    action: str | None = None,\n+) -> Cluster:\n+    \"\"\"Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    if name.startswith(\"auto/\"):\n+        raise ValueError(\n+            f\"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}\"\n+        )\n+    if name.startswith(\"epic/\"):\n+        raise ValueError(\n+            f\"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for triage epics: {name!r}\"\n+        )\n+    if name in plan[\"clusters\"]:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {name!r} already exists\")\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    cluster: Cluster = {\n+        \"name\": name,\n+        \"description\": description,\n+        \"issue_ids\": [],\n+        \"created_at\": now,\n+        \"updated_at\": now,\n+        \"auto\": False,\n+        \"cluster_key\": \"\",\n+        \"action\": action,\n+        \"user_modified\": False,\n+    }\n+    plan[\"clusters\"][name] = cluster\n+    return cluster\n+\n+\n+def add_to_cluster(\n+    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Add issue IDs to a cluster. Returns count added.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    cluster = plan[\"clusters\"].get(cluster_name)\n+    if cluster is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    member_ids: list[str] = cluster[\"issue_ids\"]\n+    count = 0\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid not in member_ids:\n+            member_ids.append(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+        # Update override to track cluster membership\n+        overrides = plan[\"overrides\"]\n+        if fid not in overrides:\n+            overrides[fid] = {\"issue_id\": fid, \"created_at\": now}\n+        overrides[fid][\"cluster\"] = cluster_name\n+        overrides[fid][\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    cluster[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def remove_from_cluster(\n+    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Remove issue IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    cluster = plan[\"clusters\"].get(cluster_name)\n+    if cluster is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    member_ids: list[str] = cluster[\"issue_ids\"]\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    count = 0\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid in member_ids:\n+            member_ids.remove(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+        # Clear cluster from override\n+        override = plan[\"overrides\"].get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\") == cluster_name:\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed\n+    if count > 0 and cluster.get(\"auto\"):\n+        cluster[\"user_modified\"] = True\n+\n+    cluster[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    cluster = plan[\"clusters\"].pop(name, None)\n+    if cluster is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    orphaned = list(cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", []))\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    for fid in orphaned:\n+        override = plan[\"overrides\"].get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\") == name:\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    if plan.get(\"active_cluster\") == name:\n+        plan[\"active_cluster\"] = None\n+\n+    return orphaned\n+\n+\n+def merge_clusters(\n+    plan: PlanModel, source_name: str, target_name: str\n+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Move all source issues to target, copy missing metadata, delete source.\n+\n+    Returns ``(added_count, source_issue_ids)``.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    if source_name == target_name:\n+        raise ValueError(\"Cannot merge a cluster into itself\")\n+    source = plan[\"clusters\"].get(source_name)\n+    if source is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Source cluster {source_name!r} does not exist\")\n+    target = plan[\"clusters\"].get(target_name)\n+    if target is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Target cluster {target_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    source_ids = list(source.get(\"issue_ids\", []))\n+    target_ids: list[str] = target[\"issue_ids\"]\n+    now = utc_now()\n+\n+    # Add source issues to target (deduplicate)\n+    existing = set(target_ids)\n+    added = 0\n+    for fid in source_ids:\n+        if fid not in existing:\n+            target_ids.append(fid)\n+            existing.add(fid)\n+            added += 1\n+        # Update override to point to target cluster\n+        overrides = plan[\"overrides\"]\n+        if fid not in overrides:\n+            overrides[fid] = {\"issue_id\": fid, \"created_at\": now}\n+        overrides[fid][\"cluster\"] = target_name\n+        overrides[fid][\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    # Copy metadata from source if target is missing them\n+    if not target.get(\"description\") and source.get(\"description\"):\n+        target[\"description\"] = source[\"description\"]\n+    if not target.get(\"action_steps\") and source.get(\"action_steps\"):\n+        target[\"action_steps\"] = list(source[\"action_steps\"])\n+    if not target.get(\"action\") and source.get(\"action\"):\n+        target[\"action\"] = source[\"action\"]\n+\n+    target[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    # Delete source cluster\n+    plan[\"clusters\"].pop(source_name, None)\n+    if plan.get(\"active_cluster\") == source_name:\n+        plan[\"active_cluster\"] = None\n+\n+    return added, source_ids\n+\n+\n+def move_cluster(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    cluster_name: str,\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    cluster = plan[\"clusters\"].get(cluster_name)\n+    if cluster is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    member_ids = list(cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", []))\n+    if not member_ids:\n+        return 0\n+\n+    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\n+    \"create_cluster\",\n+    \"add_to_cluster\",\n+    \"remove_from_cluster\",\n+    \"delete_cluster\",\n+    \"merge_clusters\",\n+    \"move_cluster\",\n+]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Sentinel: truthy so ``not plan.get(\"plan_start_scores\")`` is False,\n+    # but recognized by _seed_plan_start_scores as needing replacement.\n+    plan[\"plan_start_scores\"] = {\"reset\": True}\n+\n+\n+def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Remove issue IDs from the plan entirely.\n+\n+    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.\n+    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).\n+    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    found = 0\n+\n+    purge_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        was_present = False\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+            was_present = True\n+        if fid in skipped:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            was_present = True\n+        for cluster in plan.get(\"clusters\", {}).values():\n+            ids = cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", [])\n+            if fid in ids:\n+                ids.remove(fid)\n+                was_present = True\n+        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)\n+        override = plan.get(\"overrides\", {}).get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\"):\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = utc_now()\n+        if was_present:\n+            found += 1\n+\n+    return found\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"set_focus\", \"clear_focus\", \"reset_plan\", \"purge_ids\"]\n*** End Patch\n"

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/context_holistic/_clusters_complexity.py\n+\"\"\"Complexity hotspot builders for mechanical context.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from typing import Any\n+\n+from ._accessors import _get_detail, _get_signals, _safe_num\n+\n+\n+def _build_complexity_hotspots(\n+    by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]],\n+    by_file: dict[str, list[dict]],\n+) -> list[dict]:\n+    \"\"\"Top 20 files by composite complexity score.\"\"\"\n+    # Gather per-file structural signals\n+    file_data: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}\n+\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"structural\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        if not filepath:\n+            continue\n+        signals = _get_signals(issue)\n+        entry = file_data.setdefault(\n+            filepath,\n+            {\n+                \"file\": filepath,\n+                \"loc\": 0,\n+                \"complexity_score\": 0,\n+                \"signals\": [],\n+                \"component_count\": 0,\n+                \"function_count\": 0,\n+                \"monster_functions\": 0,\n+                \"cyclomatic_hotspots\": 0,\n+            },\n+        )\n+        entry[\"loc\"] = max(entry[\"loc\"], _safe_num(signals.get(\"loc\")))\n+        entry[\"function_count\"] = max(\n+            entry[\"function_count\"], _safe_num(signals.get(\"function_count\"))\n+        )\n+        entry[\"component_count\"] = max(\n+            entry[\"component_count\"], _safe_num(signals.get(\"component_count\"))\n+        )\n+\n+        # Collect notable signals\n+        max_params = _safe_num(signals.get(\"max_params\"))\n+        if max_params >= 5:\n+            entry[\"signals\"].append(f\"{int(max_params)} params\")\n+        max_nesting = _safe_num(signals.get(\"max_nesting\"))\n+        if max_nesting >= 4:\n+            entry[\"signals\"].append(f\"nesting depth {int(max_nesting)}\")\n+        complexity = _safe_num(signals.get(\"complexity_score\"))\n+        entry[\"complexity_score\"] = max(entry[\"complexity_score\"], complexity)\n+\n+    # Enrich with smell issues for the same files\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"smells\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        smell_id = _get_detail(issue, \"smell_id\", \"\")\n+        if filepath in file_data:\n+            if smell_id == \"monster_function\":\n+                file_data[filepath][\"monster_functions\"] += 1\n+            elif smell_id in (\"cyclomatic_complexity\", \"high_cyclomatic\"):\n+                file_data[filepath][\"cyclomatic_hotspots\"] += 1\n+\n+    # Also count responsibility_cohesion issues\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"responsibility_cohesion\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        if filepath in file_data:\n+            clusters = _safe_num(_get_detail(issue, \"cluster_count\"))\n+            file_data[filepath][\"component_count\"] = max(\n+                file_data[filepath][\"component_count\"], clusters\n+            )\n+\n+    # Composite score: loc/100 + complexity_score + component_count*3 + monster*10\n+    for entry in file_data.values():\n+        entry[\"_score\"] = (\n+            entry[\"loc\"] / 100\n+            + entry[\"complexity_score\"]\n+            + entry[\"component_count\"] * 3\n+            + entry[\"monster_functions\"] * 10\n+        )\n+        # Deduplicate signals\n+        entry[\"signals\"] = list(dict.fromkeys(entry[\"signals\"]))\n+\n+    ranked = sorted(file_data.values(), key=lambda e: -e[\"_score\"])[:20]\n+    for entry in ranked:\n+        del entry[\"_score\"]\n+    return ranked\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"_build_complexity_hotspots\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}
