__future__ import annotations\n+\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults\n+\n+\n+def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Remove a issue ID from queue_order and skipped.\"\"\"\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    if issue_id in order:\n+        order.remove(issue_id)\n+    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.\n+\n+    *issue_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative\n+    positions when they already exist in the list.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    moving = set(issue_ids or [])\n+\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        return 0\n+    if position == \"bottom\":\n+        return len(order)\n+\n+    if position in {\"before\", \"after\"} and target:\n+        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)\n+\n+    if position in {\"up\", \"down\"} and offset is not None:\n+        return _resolve_offset_position(\n+            order,\n+            moving,\n+            position=position,\n+            offset=offset,\n+            issue_ids=issue_ids,\n+        )\n+\n+    return len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_relative_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str,\n+) -> int:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):\n+        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:\n+            return i if position == \"before\" else i + 1\n+    return 0 if position == \"before\" else len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):\n+        if item_id == target:\n+            return i\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_offset_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    offset: int,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None,\n+) -> int:\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0 if position == \"up\" else len(order)\n+    first_id = issue_ids[0]\n+    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]\n+    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)\n+    if current_idx is None:\n+        if position == \"up\":\n+            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)\n+        return len(clean_order)\n+    if position == \"up\":\n+        return max(0, current_idx - offset)\n+    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)\n+\n+\n+def move_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    issue_ids: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+\n+    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered\n+    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS\n+\n+    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+\n+    # Remove from skipped if present\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+\n+    # Remove from current position in order\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+\n+    # Resolve insertion point\n+    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)\n+\n+    # Insert in original order\n+    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):\n+        order.insert(idx + i, fid)\n+\n+    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.\n+    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+        existing_promoted = set(promoted)\n+        for fid in issue_ids:\n+            if fid not in existing_promoted:\n+                promoted.append(fid)\n+                existing_promoted.add(fid)\n+        plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = promoted\n+\n+    return len(issue_ids)\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"move_items\"]\n*** End Patch\n"

Returns count skipped.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    count = 0\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    skip_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)\n+        skipped[fid] = {\n+            \"issue_id\": fid,\n+            \"kind\": kind,\n+            \"reason\": reason,\n+            \"note\": note,\n+            \"attestation\": attestation,\n+            \"created_at\": now,\n+            \"review_after\": review_after,\n+            \"skipped_at_scan\": scan_count,\n+        }\n+        count += 1\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def unskip_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.\n+\n+    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``\n+    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive\n+    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    count = 0\n+    need_reopen: list[str] = []\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+        if entry is not None:\n+            if entry.get(\"kind\") in (\"permanent\", \"false_positive\"):\n+                need_reopen.append(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+    return count, need_reopen\n+\n+\n+def resurface_stale_skips(\n+    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int\n+) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.\n+\n+    Returns list of resurfaced issue IDs.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    resurfaced: list[str] = []\n+    for fid in list(skipped):\n+        entry = skipped[fid]\n+        if entry.get(\"kind\") != \"temporary\":\n+            continue\n+        review_after = entry.get(\"review_after\")\n+        if review_after is None:\n+            continue\n+        skipped_at = entry.get(\"skipped_at_scan\", 0)\n+        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            resurfaced.append(fid)\n+    return resurfaced\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"resurface_stale_skips\", \"skip_items\", \"unskip_items\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Returns count removed.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    cluster = plan[\"clusters\"].get(cluster_name)\n+    if cluster is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    member_ids: list[str] = cluster[\"issue_ids\"]\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    count = 0\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid in member_ids:\n+            member_ids.remove(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+        # Clear cluster from override\n+        override = plan[\"overrides\"].get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\") == cluster_name:\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed\n+    if count > 0 and cluster.get(\"auto\"):\n+        cluster[\"user_modified\"] = True\n+\n+    cluster[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Add source issues to target (deduplicate)\n+    existing = set(target_ids)\n+    added = 0\n+    for fid in source_ids:\n+        if fid not in existing:\n+            target_ids.append(fid)\n+            existing.add(fid)\n+            added += 1\n+        # Sentinel: truthy so ``not plan.get(\"plan_start_scores\")`` is False,\n+    # but recognized by _seed_plan_start_scores as needing replacement.\n+    plan[\"plan_start_scores\"] = {\"reset\": True}\n+\n+\n+def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Remove issue IDs from the plan entirely.\n+\n+    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.\n+    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).\n+    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    found = 0\n+\n+    purge_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        was_present = False\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+            was_present = True\n+        if fid in skipped:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            was_present = True\n+        for cluster in plan.get(\"clusters\", {}).values():\n+            ids = cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", [])\n+            if fid in ids:\n+                ids.remove(fid)\n+                was_present = True\n+        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)\n+        override = plan.get(\"overrides\", {}).get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\"):\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = utc_now()\n+        if was_present:\n+            found += 1\n+\n+    return found\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"clear_focus\", \"purge_ids\", \"reset_plan\", \"set_focus\"]\n*** End Patch\n"

Gather per-file structural signals\n+    file_data: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}\n+\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"structural\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        if not filepath:\n+            continue\n+        signals = _get_signals(issue)\n+        entry = file_data.setdefault(\n+            filepath,\n+            {\n+                \"file\": filepath,\n+                \"loc\": 0,\n+                \"complexity_score\": 0,\n+                \"signals\": [],\n+                \"component_count\": 0,\n+                \"function_count\": 0,\n+                \"monster_functions\": 0,\n+                \"cyclomatic_hotspots\": 0,\n+            },\n+        )\n+        entry[\"loc\"] = max(entry[\"loc\"], _safe_num(signals.get(\"loc\")))\n+        entry[\"function_count\"] = max(\n+            entry[\"function_count\"], _safe_num(signals.get(\"function_count\"))\n+        )\n+        entry[\"component_count\"] = max(\n+            entry[\"component_count\"], _safe_num(signals.get(\"component_count\"))\n+        )\n+\n+        # Collect notable signals\n+        max_params = _safe_num(signals.get(\"max_params\"))\n+        if max_params >= 5:\n+            entry[\"signals\"].append(f\"{int(max_params)} params\")\n+        max_nesting = _safe_num(signals.get(\"max_nesting\"))\n+        if max_nesting >= 4:\n+            entry[\"signals\"].append(f\"nesting depth {int(max_nesting)}\")\n+        complexity = _safe_num(signals.get(\"complexity_score\"))\n+        entry[\"complexity_score\"] = max(entry[\"complexity_score\"], complexity)\n+\n+    # Enrich with smell issues for the same files\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"smells\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        smell_id = _get_detail(issue, \"smell_id\", \"\")\n+        if filepath in file_data:\n+            if smell_id == \"monster_function\":\n+                file_data[filepath][\"monster_functions\"] += 1\n+            elif smell_id in (\"cyclomatic_complexity\", \"high_cyclomatic\"):\n+                file_data[filepath][\"cyclomatic_hotspots\"] += 1\n+\n+    # Also count responsibility_cohesion issues\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"responsibility_cohesion\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        if filepath in file_data:\n+            clusters = _safe_num(_get_detail(issue, \"cluster_count\"))\n+            file_data[filepath][\"component_count\"] = max(\n+                file_data[filepath][\"component_count\"], clusters\n+            )\n+\n+    # Composite score: loc/100 + complexity_score + component_count*3 + monster*10\n+    for entry in file_data.values():\n+        entry[\"_score\"] = (\n+            entry[\"loc\"] / 100\n+            + entry[\"complexity_score\"]\n+            + entry[\"component_count\"] * 3\n+            + entry[\"monster_functions\"] * 10\n+        )\n+        # Deduplicate signals\n+        entry[\"signals\"] = list(dict.fromkeys(entry[\"signals\"]))\n+\n+    ranked = sorted(file_data.values(), key=lambda e: -e[\"_score\"])[:20]\n+    for entry in ranked:\n+        del entry[\"_score\"]\n+    return ranked\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"_build_complexity_hotspots\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

results.sort(key=lambda e: (-e[\"high_severity\"], -e[\"total\"]))\n+    return results[:20]\n+\n+\n+def _build_signal_density(by_file: dict[str, list[dict]]) -> list[dict]:\n+    \"\"\"Top 20 files by number of distinct detectors firing.\"\"\"\n+    results: list[dict] = []\n+    for filepath, file_issues in by_file.items():\n+        detectors = set()\n+        for issue in file_issues:\n+            det = issue.get(\"detector\", \"\")\n+            if det:\n+                detectors.add(det)\n+        if len(detectors) >= 2:\n+            results.append(\n+                {\n+   "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/context_holistic/mechanical.py\n+\"\"\"Aggregate mechanical detector issues into structured evidence clusters.\n+\n+Reads ALL state issues and produces signal clusters organized for holistic\n+review context enrichment. The LLM reviewer gets hotspots and patterns —\n+not raw issue dumps — so it can investigate root causes.\n+\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from collections import Counter, defaultdict\n+from typing import Any\n+\n+from ._clusters_complexity import _build_complexity_hotspots\n+from ._clusters_consistency import _build_duplicate_clusters, _build_naming_drift\n+from ._clusters_dependency import (\n+    _build_boundary_violations,\n+    _build_dead_code,\n+    _build_deferred_import_density,\n+    _build_private_crossings,\n+)\n+from ._clusters_error_state import _build_error_hotspots, _build_mutable_globals\n+from ._clusters_organization import _build_flat_dir_issues, _build_large_file_distribution\n+from ._clusters_security import (\n+    _build_security_hotspots,\n+    _build_signal_density,\n+    _build_systemic_patterns,\n+)\n+\n+\n+def _normalize_allowed_files(\n+    allowed_files: set[str] | list[str] | tuple[str, ...] | None,\n+) -> set[str] | None:\n+    \"\"\"Normalize optional allowed-file scope to slash-normalized relative paths.\"\"\"\n+    if allowed_files is None:\n+        return None\n+    out: set[str] = set()\n+    for raw in allowed_files:\n+        if not isinstance(raw, str):\n+            continue\n+        file_path = raw.strip().replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\")\n+        if file_path:\n+            out.add(file_path)\n+    return out\n+\n+\n+def gather_mechanical_evidence(\n+    state: dict[str, Any],\n+    *,\n+    allowed_files: set[str] | list[str] | tuple[str, ...] | None = None,\n+) -> dict[str, Any]:\n+    \"\"\"Aggregate open issues into evidence clusters for holistic review.\n+\n+    Returns a dict of named clusters. Empty dict when no issues exist.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    issues = state.get(\"issues\", {})\n+    if not issues:\n+        return {}\n+    allowed_scope = _normalize_allowed_files(allowed_files)\n+\n+    # Single-pass bucketing\n+    by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)\n+    by_file: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)\n+    smell_counter: Counter[str] = Counter()\n+    smell_files: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)  # smell_id -> [files]\n+\n+    for issue in issues.values():\n+        if not isinstance(issue, dict):\n+            continue\n+        if issue.get(\"status\") != \"open\":\n+            continue\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        normalized_file = filepath.strip().replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\") if isinstance(filepath, str) else \"\"\n+        if allowed_scope is not None and normalized_file not in allowed_scope:\n+            continue\n+        det = issue.get(\"detector\", \"\")\n+        if det:\n+            by_detector[det].append(issue)\n+        if normalized_file and normalized_file != \".\":\n+            by_file[normalized_file].append(issue)\n+        # Track smell subtypes for systemic pattern detection\n+        if det == \"smells\":\n+            detail = issue.get(\"detail\", {})\n+            smell_id = detail.get(\"smell_id\", \"\") if isinstance(detail, dict) else \"\"\n+            if smell_id:\n+                smell_counter[smell_id] += 1\n+                smell_files[smell_id].append(normalized_file)\n+\n+    if not by_detector:\n+        return {}\n+\n+    evidence: dict[str, Any] = {}\n+\n+    # Cluster A: Structural Complexity\n+    complexity_hotspots = _build_complexity_hotspots(by_detector, by_file)\n+    if complexity_hotspots:\n+        evidence[\"complexity_hotspots\"] = complexity_hotspots\n+\n+    # Cluster B: Error & State Hygiene\n+    error_hotspots = _build_error_hotspots(by_detector)\n+    if error_hotspots:\n+        evidence[\"error_hotspots\"] = error_hotspots\n+\n+    mutable_globals = _build_mutable_globals(by_detector)\n+    if mutable_globals:\n+        evidence[\"mutable_globals\"] = mutable_globals\n+\n+    # Cluster C: Dependency Architecture\n+    boundary_violations = _build_boundary_violations(by_detector)\n+    if boundary_violations:\n+        evidence[\"boundary_violations\"] = boundary_violations\n+\n+    dead_code = _build_dead_code(by_detector)\n+    if dead_code:\n+        evidence[\"dead_code\"] = dead_code\n+\n+    private_crossings = _build_private_crossings(by_detector)\n+    if private_crossings:\n+        evidence[\"private_crossings\"] = private_crossings\n+\n+    deferred = _build_deferred_import_density(by_file)\n+    if deferred:\n+        evidence[\"deferred_import_density\"] = deferred\n+\n+    # Cluster D: Consistency & Duplication\n+    duplicate_clusters = _build_duplicate_clusters(by_detector)\n+    if duplicate_clusters:\n+        evidence[\"duplicate_clusters\"] = duplicate_clusters\n+\n+    naming_drift = _build_naming_drift(by_detector)\n+    if naming_drift:\n+        evidence[\"naming_drift\"] = naming_drift\n+\n+    # Cluster E: Organization\n+    flat_dir_issues = _build_flat_dir_issues(by_detector)\n+    if flat_dir_issues:\n+        evidence[\"flat_dir_issues\"] = flat_dir_issues\n+\n+    large_dist = _build_large_file_distribution(by_detector)\n+    if large_dist:\n+        evidence[\"large_file_distribution\"] = large_dist\n+\n+    # Cluster F: Security Density\n+    security_hotspots = _build_security_hotspots(by_detector)\n+    if security_hotspots:\n+        evidence[\"security_hotspots\"] = security_hotspots\n+\n+    # Cross-cutting: Signal density\n+    signal_density = _build_signal_density(by_file)\n+    if signal_density:\n+        evidence[\"signal_density\"] = signal_density\n+\n+    # Systemic patterns\n+    systemic = _build_systemic_patterns(smell_counter, smell_files)\n+    if systemic:\n+        evidence[\"systemic_patterns\"] = systemic\n+\n+    return evidence\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"gather_mechanical_evidence\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

queue_order and skipped.\"\"\"\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    if issue_id in order:\n+        order.remove(issue_id)\n+    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.\n+\n+    *issue_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative\n+    positions when they already exist in the list.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    moving = set(issue_ids or [])\n+\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        return 0\n+    if position == \"bottom\":\n+        return len(order)\n+\n+    if position in {\"before\", \"after\"} and target:\n+        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)\n+\n+    if position in {\"up\", \"down\"} and offset is not None:\n+        return _resolve_offset_position(\n+            order,\n+            moving,\n+            position=position,\n+            offset=offset,\n+            issue_ids=issue_ids,\n+        )\n+\n+    return len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_relative_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str,\n+) -> int:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):\n+        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:\n+            return i if position == \"before\" else i + 1\n+    return 0 if position == \"before\" else len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):\n+        if item_id == target:\n+            return i\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_offset_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    offset: int,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None,\n+) -> int:\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0 if position == \"up\" else len(order)\n+    first_id = issue_ids[0]\n+    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]\n+    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)\n+    if current_idx is None:\n+        if position == \"up\":\n+            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)\n+        return len(clean_order)\n+    if position == \"up\":\n+        return max(0, current_idx - offset)\n+    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)\n+\n+\n+def move_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    issue_ids: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+\n+    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered\n+    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS\n+\n+    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+\n+    # Remove from skipped if present\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+\n+    # Remove from current position in order\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+\n+    # Resolve insertion point\n+    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)\n+\n+    # Insert in original order\n+    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):\n+        order.insert(idx + i, fid)\n+\n+    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.\n+    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+        existing_promoted = set(promoted)\n+        for fid in issue_ids:\n+            if fid not in existing_promoted:\n+                promoted.append(fid)\n+                existing_promoted.add(fid)\n+        plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = promoted\n+\n+    return len(issue_ids)\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"move_items\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_skip.py\n+\"\"\"Skip/unskip mutations for plan operations.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_queue import _remove_id_from_lists\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SkipEntry, ensure_plan_defaults\n+from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now\n+\n+\n+def skip_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    issue_ids: list[str],\n+    *,\n+    kind: str = \"temporary\",\n+    reason: str | None = None,\n+    note: str | None = None,\n+    attestation: str | None = None,\n+    review_after: int | None = None,\n+    scan_count: int = 0,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move issue IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    count = 0\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    skip_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)\n+        skipped[fid] = {\n+            \"issue_id\": fid,\n+            \"kind\": kind,\n+            \"reason\": reason,\n+            \"note\": note,\n+            \"attestation\": attestation,\n+            \"created_at\": now,\n+            \"review_after\": review_after,\n+            \"skipped_at_scan\": scan_count,\n+        }\n+        count += 1\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def unskip_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.\n+\n+    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``\n+    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive\n+    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    count = 0\n+    need_reopen: list[str] = []\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+        if entry is not None:\n+            if entry.get(\"kind\") in (\"permanent\", \"false_positive\"):\n+                need_reopen.append(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+    return count, need_reopen\n+\n+\n+def resurface_stale_skips(\n+    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int\n+) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.\n+\n+    Returns list of resurfaced issue IDs.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    resurfaced: list[str] = []\n+    for fid in list(skipped):\n+        entry = skipped[fid]\n+        if entry.get(\"kind\") != \"temporary\":\n+            continue\n+        review_after = entry.get(\"review_after\")\n+        if review_after is None:\n+            continue\n+        skipped_at = entry.get(\"skipped_at_scan\", 0)\n+        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            resurfaced.append(fid)\n+    return resurfaced\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"resurface_stale_skips\", \"skip_items\", \"unskip_items\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_cluster.py\n+\"\"\"Cluster mutations for plan operations.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.operations_queue import move_items\n+from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults\n+from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now\n+\n+\n+def create_cluster(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    name: str,\n+    description: str | None = None,\n+    action: str | None = None,\n+) -> Cluster:\n+    \"\"\"Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    if name.startswith(\"auto/\"):\n+        raise ValueError(\n+            f\"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}\"\n+        )\n+    if name.startswith(\"epic/\"):\n+        raise ValueError(\n+            f\"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for triage epics: {name!r}\"\n+        )\n+    if name in plan[\"clusters\"]:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {name!r} already exists\")\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    cluster: Cluster = {\n+        \"name\": name,\n+        \"description\": description,\n+        \"issue_ids\": [],\n+        \"created_at\": now,\n+        \"updated_at\": now,\n+        \"auto\": False,\n+        \"cluster_key\": \"\",\n+        \"action\": action,\n+        \"user_modified\": False,\n+    }\n+    plan[\"clusters\"][name] = cluster\n+    return cluster\n+\n+\n+def add_to_cluster(\n+    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Add issue IDs to a cluster. Returns count added.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    cluster = plan[\"clusters\"].get(cluster_name)\n+    if cluster is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    member_ids: list[str] = cluster[\"issue_ids\"]\n+    count = 0\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid not in member_ids:\n+            member_ids.append(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+        # Update override to track cluster membership\n+        overrides = plan[\"overrides\"]\n+        if fid not in overrides:\n+            overrides[fid] = {\"issue_id\": fid, \"created_at\": now}\n+        overrides[fid][\"cluster\"] = cluster_name\n+        overrides[fid][\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    cluster[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def remove_from_cluster(\n+    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Remove issue IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    cluster = plan[\"clusters\"].get(cluster_name)\n+    if cluster is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    member_ids: list[str] = cluster[\"issue_ids\"]\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    count = 0\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid in member_ids:\n+            member_ids.remove(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+        # Clear cluster from override\n+        override = plan[\"overrides\"].get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\") == cluster_name:\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed\n+    if count > 0 and cluster.get(\"auto\"):\n+        cluster[\"user_modified\"] = True\n+\n+    cluster[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    cluster = plan[\"clusters\"].pop(name, None)\n+    if cluster is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    orphaned = list(cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", []))\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    for fid in orphaned:\n+        override = plan[\"overrides\"].get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\") == name:\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    if plan.get(\"active_cluster\") == name:\n+        plan[\"active_cluster\"] = None\n+\n+    return orphaned\n+\n+\n+def merge_clusters(\n+    plan: PlanModel, source_name: str, target_name: str\n+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Move all source issues to target, copy missing metadata, delete source.\n+\n+    Returns ``(added_count, source_issue_ids)``.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    if source_name == target_name:\n+        raise ValueError(\"Cannot merge a cluster into itself\")\n+    source = plan[\"clusters\"].get(source_name)\n+    if source is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Source cluster {source_name!r} does not exist\")\n+    target = plan[\"clusters\"].get(target_name)\n+    if target is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Target cluster {target_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    source_ids = list(source.get(\"issue_ids\", []))\n+    target_ids: list[str] = target[\"issue_ids\"]\n+    now = utc_now()\n+\n+    # Add source issues to target (deduplicate)\n+    existing = set(target_ids)\n+    added = 0\n+    for fid in source_ids:\n+        if fid not in existing:\n+            target_ids.append(fid)\n+            existing.add(fid)\n+            added += 1\n+        # Update override to point to target cluster\n+        overrides = plan[\"overrides\"]\n+        if fid not in overrides:\n+            overrides[fid] = {\"issue_id\": fid, \"created_at\": now}\n+        overrides[fid][\"cluster\"] = target_name\n+        overrides[fid][\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    # Copy metadata from source if target is missing them\n+    if not target.get(\"description\") and source.get(\"description\"):\n+        target[\"description\"] = source[\"description\"]\n+    if not target.get(\"action_steps\") and source.get(\"action_steps\"):\n+        target[\"action_steps\"] = list(source[\"action_steps\"])\n+    if not target.get(\"action\") and source.get(\"action\"):\n+        target[\"action\"] = source[\"action\"]\n+\n+    target[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    # Delete source cluster\n+    plan[\"clusters\"].pop(source_name, None)\n+    if plan.get(\"active_cluster\") == source_name:\n+        plan[\"active_cluster\"] = None\n+\n+    return added, source_ids\n+\n+\n+def move_cluster(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    cluster_name: str,\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    cluster = plan[\"clusters\"].get(cluster_name)\n+    if cluster is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    member_ids = list(cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", []))\n+    if not member_ids:\n+        return 0\n+\n+    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\n+    \"add_to_cluster\",\n+    \"create_cluster\",\n+    \"delete_cluster\",\n+    \"merge_clusters\",\n+    \"move_cluster\",\n+    \"remove_from_cluster\",\n+]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

\"\"\"Set or clear a note on a issue.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    overrides = plan[\"overrides\"]\n+    if issue_id not in overrides:\n+        overrides[issue_id] = {\"issue_id\": issue_id, \"created_at\": now}\n+    overrides[issue_id][\"note\"] = note\n+    overrides[issue_id][\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"annotate_issue\", \"append_log_entry\", \"describe_issue\"]\n*** End Patch\n"

Sentinel: truthy so ``not plan.get(\"plan_start_scores\")`` is False,\n+    # but recognized by _seed_plan_start_scores as needing replacement.\n+    plan[\"plan_start_scores\"] = {\"reset\": True}\n+\n+\n+def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Remove issue IDs from the plan entirely.\n+\n+    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.\n+    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).\n+    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    found = 0\n+\n+    purge_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        was_present = False\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+            was_present = True\n+        if fid in skipped:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            was_present = True\n+        for cluster in plan.get(\"clusters\", {}).values():\n+            ids = cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", [])\n+            if fid in ids:\n+                ids.remove(fid)\n+                was_present = True\n+        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)\n+        override = plan.get(\"overrides\", {}).get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\"):\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = utc_now()\n+        if was_present:\n+            found += 1\n+\n+    return found\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"clear_focus\", \"purge_ids\", \"reset_plan\", \"set_focus\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Fall back to ``detail`` itself when the nested\n+    ``signals`` key is absent so hotspot aggregation sees real values.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    detail = issue.get(\"detail\", {})\n+    if not isinstance(detail, dict):\n+        return {}\n+    signals = detail.get(\"signals\")\n+    if isinstance(signals, dict):\n+        return signals\n+    return detail\n+\n+\n+def _safe_num(value: Any, default: float = 0.0) -> float:\n+    if isinstance(value, bool):\n+        return default\n+    if isinstance(value, int | float):\n+        return float(value)\n+    return default\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"_get_detail\", \"_get_signals\", \"_safe_num\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: desloppify/intelligence/review/context_holistic/_clusters_complexity.py\n+\"\"\"Complexity-focused mechanical evidence clusters.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+from typing import Any\n+\n+from ._accessors import _get_detail, _get_signals, _safe_num\n+\n+\n+def _build_complexity_hotspots(\n+    by_detector: dict[str, list[dict]],\n+    by_file: dict[str, list[dict]],\n+) -> list[dict]:\n+    \"\"\"Top 20 files by composite complexity score.\"\"\"\n+    del by_file\n+    # Gather per-file structural signals\n+    file_data: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}\n+\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"structural\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        if not filepath:\n+            continue\n+        signals = _get_signals(issue)\n+        entry = file_data.setdefault(\n+            filepath,\n+            {\n+                \"file\": filepath,\n+                \"loc\": 0,\n+                \"complexity_score\": 0,\n+                \"signals\": [],\n+                \"component_count\": 0,\n+                \"function_count\": 0,\n+                \"monster_functions\": 0,\n+                \"cyclomatic_hotspots\": 0,\n+            },\n+        )\n+        entry[\"loc\"] = max(entry[\"loc\"], _safe_num(signals.get(\"loc\")))\n+        entry[\"function_count\"] = max(\n+            entry[\"function_count\"], _safe_num(signals.get(\"function_count\"))\n+        )\n+        entry[\"component_count\"] = max(\n+            entry[\"component_count\"], _safe_num(signals.get(\"component_count\"))\n+        )\n+\n+        # Collect notable signals\n+        max_params = _safe_num(signals.get(\"max_params\"))\n+        if max_params >= 5:\n+            entry[\"signals\"].append(f\"{int(max_params)} params\")\n+        max_nesting = _safe_num(signals.get(\"max_nesting\"))\n+        if max_nesting >= 4:\n+            entry[\"signals\"].append(f\"nesting depth {int(max_nesting)}\")\n+        complexity = _safe_num(signals.get(\"complexity_score\"))\n+        entry[\"complexity_score\"] = max(entry[\"complexity_score\"], complexity)\n+\n+    # Enrich with smell issues for the same files\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"smells\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        smell_id = _get_detail(issue, \"smell_id\", \"\")\n+        if filepath in file_data:\n+            if smell_id == \"monster_function\":\n+                file_data[filepath][\"monster_functions\"] += 1\n+            elif smell_id in (\"cyclomatic_complexity\", \"high_cyclomatic\"):\n+                file_data[filepath][\"cyclomatic_hotspots\"] += 1\n+\n+    # Also count responsibility_cohesion issues\n+    for issue in by_detector.get(\"responsibility_cohesion\", []):\n+        filepath = issue.get(\"file\", \"\")\n+        if filepath in file_data:\n+            clusters = _safe_num(_get_detail(issue, \"cluster_count\"))\n+            file_data[filepath][\"component_count\"] = max(\n+                file_data[filepath][\"component_count\"], clusters\n+            )\n+\n+    # Composite score: loc/100 + complexity_score + component_count*3 + monster*10\n+    for entry in file_data.values():\n+        entry[\"_score\"] = (\n+            entry[\"loc\"] / 100\n+            + entry[\"complexity_score\"]\n+            + entry[\"component_count\"] * 3\n+            + entry[\"monster_functions\"] * 10\n+        )\n+        # Deduplicate signals\n+        entry[\"signals\"] = list(dict.fromkeys(entry[\"signals\"]))\n+\n+    ranked = sorted(file_data.values(), key=lambda e: -e[\"_score\"])[:20]\n+    for entry in ranked:\n+        del entry[\"_score\"]\n+    return ranked\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"_build_complexity_hotspots\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

\"deferred_import\":\n+                file_counts[filepath] += 1\n+\n+    results = [\n+        {\"file\": filepath, \"count\": count}\n+        for filepath, count in file_counts.items()\n+  \"\"\"Remove a issue ID from queue_order and skipped.\"\"\"\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    if issue_id in order:\n+        order.remove(issue_id)\n+    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.\n+\n+    *issue_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative\n+    positions when they already exist in the list.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    moving = set(issue_ids or [])\n+\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        return 0\n+    if position == \"bottom\":\n+        return len(order)\n+\n+    if position in {\"before\", \"after\"} and target:\n+        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)\n+\n+    if position in {\"up\", \"down\"} and offset is not None:\n+        return _resolve_offset_position(\n+            order,\n+            moving,\n+            position=position,\n+            offset=offset,\n+            issue_ids=issue_ids,\n+        )\n+\n+    return len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_relative_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str,\n+) -> int:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):\n+        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:\n+            return i if position == \"before\" else i + 1\n+    return 0 if position == \"before\" else len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):\n+        if item_id == target:\n+            return i\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_offset_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    offset: int,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None,\n+) -> int:\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0 if position == \"up\" else len(order)\n+    first_id = issue_ids[0]\n+    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]\n+    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)\n+    if current_idx is None:\n+        if position == \"up\":\n+            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)\n+        return len(clean_order)\n+    if position == \"up\":\n+        return max(0, current_idx - offset)\n+    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)\n+\n+\n+def move_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    issue_ids: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+\n+    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered\n+    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS\n+\n+    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+\n+    # Remove from skipped if present\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+\n+    # Remove from current position in order\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+\n+    # Resolve insertion point\n+    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)\n+\n+    # Insert in original order\n+    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):\n+        order.insert(idx + i, fid)\n+\n+    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.\n+    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+        existing_promoted = set(promoted)\n+        for fid in issue_ids:\n+            if fid not in existing_promoted:\n+                promoted.append(fid)\n+                existing_promoted.add(fid)\n+        plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = promoted\n+\n+    return len(issue_ids)\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"move_items\"]\n*** End Patch\n"

Returns count skipped.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    count = 0\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    skip_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)\n+        skipped[fid] = {\n+            \"issue_id\": fid,\n+            \"kind\": kind,\n+            \"reason\": reason,\n+            \"note\": note,\n+            \"attestation\": attestation,\n+            \"created_at\": now,\n+            \"review_after\": review_after,\n+            \"skipped_at_scan\": scan_count,\n+        }\n+        count += 1\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def unskip_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.\n+\n+    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``\n+    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive\n+    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    count = 0\n+    need_reopen: list[str] = []\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+        if entry is not None:\n+            if entry.get(\"kind\") in (\"permanent\", \"false_positive\"):\n+                need_reopen.append(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+    return count, need_reopen\n+\n+\n+def resurface_stale_skips(\n+    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int\n+) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.\n+\n+    Returns list of resurfaced issue IDs.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    resurfaced: list[str] = []\n+    for fid in list(skipped):\n+        entry = skipped[fid]\n+        if entry.get(\"kind\") != \"temporary\":\n+            continue\n+        review_after = entry.get(\"review_after\")\n+        if review_after is None:\n+            continue\n+        skipped_at = entry.get(\"skipped_at_scan\", 0)\n+        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            resurfaced.append(fid)\n+    return resurfaced\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"resurface_stale_skips\", \"skip_items\", \"unskip_items\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

exist\")\n+\n+    orphaned = list(cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", []))\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    for fid in orphaned:\n+        override = plan[\"overrides\"].get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\") == name:\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    if plan.get(\"active_cluster\") == name:\n+        plan[\"active_cluster\"] = None\n+\n+    return orphaned\n+\n+\n+def merge_clusters(\n+    plan: PlanModel, source_name: str, target_name: str\n+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Move all source issues to target, copy missing metadata, delete source.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    if source_name == target_name:\n+        raise ValueError(\"Cannot merge a cluster into itself\")\n+    source = plan[\"clusters\"].get(source_name)\n+    if source is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Source cluster {source_name!r} does not exist\")\n+    target = plan[\"clusters\"].get(target_name)\n+    if target is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Target cluster {target_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    source_ids = list(source.get(\"issue_ids\", []))\n+    target_ids: list[str] = target[\"issue_ids\"]\n+    now = utc_now()\n+\n+    existing = set(target_ids)\n+    added = 0\n+    for fid in source_ids:\n+        if fid not in existing:\n+            target_ids.append(fid)\n+            existing.add(fid)\n+            added += 1\n+        overrides = plan[\"overrides\"]\n+        if fid not in overrides:\n+            overrides[fid] = {\"issue_id\": fid, \"created_at\": now}\n+        overrides[fid][\"cluster\"] = target_name\n+        overrides[fid][\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    if not target.get(\"description\") and source.get(\"description\"):\n+        target[\"description\"] = source[\"description\"]\n+    if not target.get(\"action_steps\") and source.get(\"action_steps\"):\n+        target[\"action_steps\"] = list(source[\"action_steps\"])\n+    if not target.get(\"action\") and source.get(\"action\"):\n+        target[\"action\"] = source[\"action\"]\n+\n+    target[\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+    plan[\"clusters\"].pop(source_name, None)\n+    if plan.get(\"active_cluster\") == source_name:\n+        plan[\"active_cluster\"] = None\n+\n+    return added, source_ids\n+\n+\n+def move_cluster(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    cluster_name: str,\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    cluster = plan[\"clusters\"].get(cluster_name)\n+    if cluster is None:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+\n+    member_ids = list(cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", []))\n+    if not member_ids:\n+        return 0\n+\n+    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\n+    \"add_to_cluster\",\n+    \"create_cluster\",\n+    \"delete_cluster\",\n+    \"merge_clusters\",\n+    \"move_cluster\",\n+    \"remove_from_cluster\",\n+]\n*** End Patch\n"

override = plan.get(\"overrides\", {}).get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\"):\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = utc_now()\n+ entry[\"signals\"].append(f\"{int(max_params)} params\")\n+        max_nesting = _safe_num(signals.get(\"max_nesting\"))\n+        if max_nesting >= 4:\n+            entry[\"signals\"].append(f\"nesting depth {int(max_nesting)}\")\n+        complexity = _safe_num(signals.get(\"complexity_score\"))\n+        entry[\"complexity_score\"] =        )\n+        entry[\"signals\"] = list(dict.fromkeys(entry[\"signals\"]))\n+\n+    ranked = sorted(file_data.values(), key=lambda e: -e[\"_score\"])[:20]\n+    for entry in ranked:\n+        del  \"\"\"Remove a issue ID from queue_order and skipped.\"\"\"\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    if issue_id in order:\n+        order.remove(issue_id)\n+    skipped.pop(issue_id, None)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.\n+\n+    *issue_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative\n+    positions when they already exist in the list.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    moving = set(issue_ids or [])\n+\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        return 0\n+    if position == \"bottom\":\n+        return len(order)\n+\n+    if position in {\"before\", \"after\"} and target:\n+        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)\n+\n+    if position in {\"up\", \"down\"} and offset is not None:\n+        return _resolve_offset_position(\n+            order,\n+            moving,\n+            position=position,\n+            offset=offset,\n+            issue_ids=issue_ids,\n+        )\n+\n+    return len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_relative_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str,\n+) -> int:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):\n+        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:\n+            return i if position == \"before\" else i + 1\n+    return 0 if position == \"before\" else len(order)\n+\n+\n+def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:\n+    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):\n+        if item_id == target:\n+            return i\n+    return None\n+\n+\n+def _resolve_offset_position(\n+    order: list[str],\n+    moving: set[str],\n+    *,\n+    position: str,\n+    offset: int,\n+    issue_ids: list[str] | None,\n+) -> int:\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0 if position == \"up\" else len(order)\n+    first_id = issue_ids[0]\n+    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]\n+    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)\n+    if current_idx is None:\n+        if position == \"up\":\n+            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)\n+        return len(clean_order)\n+    if position == \"up\":\n+        return max(0, current_idx - offset)\n+    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)\n+\n+\n+def move_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel,\n+    issue_ids: list[str],\n+    position: str,\n+    target: str | None = None,\n+    offset: int | None = None,\n+) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Move issue IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+\n+    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered\n+    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS\n+\n+    issue_ids = [fid for fid in issue_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]\n+    if not issue_ids:\n+        return 0\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+\n+    # Remove from skipped if present\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get(\"skipped\", {})\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+\n+    # Remove from current position in order\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+\n+    # Resolve insertion point\n+    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, issue_ids)\n+\n+    # Insert in original order\n+    for i, fid in enumerate(issue_ids):\n+        order.insert(idx + i, fid)\n+\n+    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.\n+    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.\n+    if position == \"top\":\n+        promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+        existing_promoted = set(promoted)\n+        for fid in issue_ids:\n+            if fid not in existing_promoted:\n+                promoted.append(fid)\n+                existing_promoted.add(fid)\n+        plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = promoted\n+\n+    return len(issue_ids)\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"move_items\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Returns count skipped.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    count = 0\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    skip_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)\n+        skipped[fid] = {\n+            \"issue_id\": fid,\n+            \"kind\": kind,\n+            \"reason\": reason,\n+            \"note\": note,\n+            \"attestation\": attestation,\n+            \"created_at\": now,\n+            \"review_after\": review_after,\n+            \"skipped_at_scan\": scan_count,\n+        }\n+        count += 1\n+    return count\n+\n+\n+def unskip_items(\n+    plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]\n+) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:\n+    \"\"\"Bring issue IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.\n+\n+    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``\n+    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive\n+    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    count = 0\n+    need_reopen: list[str] = []\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)\n+        if entry is not None:\n+            if entry.get(\"kind\") in (\"permanent\", \"false_positive\"):\n+                need_reopen.append(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            count += 1\n+    return count, need_reopen\n+\n+\n+def resurface_stale_skips(\n+    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int\n+) -> list[str]:\n+    \"\"\"Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.\n+\n+    Returns list of resurfaced issue IDs.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    resurfaced: list[str] = []\n+    for fid in list(skipped):\n+        entry = skipped[fid]\n+        if entry.get(\"kind\") != \"temporary\":\n+            continue\n+        review_after = entry.get(\"review_after\")\n+        if review_after is None:\n+            continue\n+        skipped_at = entry.get(\"skipped_at_scan\", 0)\n+        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            if fid not in plan[\"queue_order\"]:\n+                plan[\"queue_order\"].append(fid)\n+            resurfaced.append(fid)\n+    return resurfaced\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"resurface_stale_skips\", \"skip_items\", \"unskip_items\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

overrides[issue_id][\"description\"] = description\n+    overrides[issue_id][\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+\n+def annotate_issue(\n+    plan: PlanModel, issue_id: str, note: str | None\n+) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Set or clear a note on a issue.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    now = utc_now()\n+    overrides = plan[\"overrides\"]\n+    if issue_id not in overrides:\n+        overrides[issue_id] = {\"issue_id\": issue_id, \"created_at\": now}\n+    overrides[issue_id][\"note\"] = note\n+    overrides[issue_id][\"updated_at\"] = now\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"annotate_issue\", \"append_log_entry\", \"describe_issue\"]\n*** End Patch\n"
}

plan[\"clusters\"]:\n+        raise ValueError(f\"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist\")\n+    plan[\"active_cluster\"] = cluster_name\n+\n+\n+def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Clear the active cluster focus.\"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    plan[\"active_cluster\"] = None\n+\n+\n+def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:\n+    \"\"\"Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp.\n+\n+    Sets ``plan_start_scores`` to a sentinel so the next scan seeds real\n+    scores instead of incorrectly treating the reset as a completed cycle.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    created = plan.get(\"created\", utc_now())\n+    plan.clear()\n+    for k, v in empty_plan().items():\n+        plan[k] = v\n+    plan[\"created\"] = created\n+    plan[\"plan_start_scores\"] = {\"reset\": True}\n+\n+\n+def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int:\n+    \"\"\"Remove issue IDs from the plan entirely.\n+\n+    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.\n+    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).\n+    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.\n+    \"\"\"\n+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)\n+    found = 0\n+\n+    purge_set = set(issue_ids)\n+    promoted: list[str] = plan.get(\"promoted_ids\", [])\n+    plan[\"promoted_ids\"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]\n+\n+    order: list[str] = plan[\"queue_order\"]\n+    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan[\"skipped\"]\n+    for fid in issue_ids:\n+        was_present = False\n+        if fid in order:\n+            order.remove(fid)\n+            was_present = True\n+        if fid in skipped:\n+            skipped.pop(fid)\n+            was_present = True\n+        for cluster in plan.get(\"clusters\", {}).values():\n+            ids = cluster.get(\"issue_ids\", [])\n+            if fid in ids:\n+                ids.remove(fid)\n+                was_present = True\n+        override = plan.get(\"overrides\", {}).get(fid)\n+        if override and override.get(\"cluster\"):\n+            override[\"cluster\"] = None\n+            override[\"updated_at\"] = utc_now()\n+        if was_present:\n+            found += 1\n+\n+    return found\n+\n+\n+__all__ = [\"clear_focus\", \"purge_ids\", \"reset_plan\", \"set_focus\"]\n*** End Patch\n"

> AGENT

The triage split exposed a compatibility gap: tests monkeypatch `triage_handlers` symbols (`load_plan`, `command_runtime`, `save_plan`) expecting those patches to affect all stage logic, which no longer happened after modularization. I’m adding a binding-sync layer in `triage_handlers.py` so patched symbols are propagated into `triage/*` modules before dispatch, restoring behavior while keeping the split.
